    与铁旋转序耦合的轴向声子之非互易圆二色性#
    Non-reciprocal circular dichroism of axial phonons coupled to ferro-rotational order#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 H. Y. Huang, G. Channagowdra, D. Banerjee, E. V. Komleva, J. Okamoto, C. T. Chen, M. Guennou, S. Johnston, S. V. Streltsov, C. Y. Mou, A. Fujimori, S-W. Cheong, D. J. Huang

    📄 摘要：X 射线吸收中的圆二色性（CD）定义为对相反圆偏振光的吸收差异，其根本上由时间反演对称性或反演对称性的破缺所允许，因此对磁性、手性及其相互作用十分敏感。相较之下，仅靠样品对称性并不足以决定共振非弹性 X 射线散射（RIXS）中是否允许出现 CD。实际上，RIXS-CD 同时受样品对称性和散射几何的支配。本文利用 RIXS，在 MnTiO3 中识别出与铁旋转序耦合的圆偏振声子，我们将其称为铁轴向声子（ferro-axial phonons）。这些激发直接展示了非互易 RIXS-CD，即当反转入射 X 射线的传播方向时，二色响应会发生变化，尽管体系整体同时保持反演和时间反演对称性。我们提出，这些声子的凝聚态表现为驻波，可能是 MnTiO3 中铁旋转序的根源。所观测到的非互易 CD 反映了光子螺旋度、声子极化和铁旋转序之间的相互作用。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2512.22050v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Circular dichroism (CD) in X-ray absorption, defined as the difference in absorption between opposite circular polarizations, is fundamentally enabled by the breaking of time-reversal symmetry or inversion symmetry. It is therefore sensitive to magnetism, chirality, and their interplay. In contrast, the sample symmetry alone is insufficient to determine whether CD in resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) is allowed. Rather, RIXS-CD is governed by both the sample symmetry and the scattering geometry. Here, using RIXS, we identify circularly polarized phonons coupled to ferro-rotational order in MnTiO₃, which we refer to as ferro-axial phonons. Their excitations provide a direct demonstration of non-reciprocal RIXS-CD, in which the dichroic response changes upon reversing the propagation direction of the incident X-rays, although the system globally preserves both inversion and time-reversal symmetries. We propose that a condensate of these phonons, manifested as standing waves, underlies the ferro-rotational order in MnTiO₃. The observed non-reciprocal CD reflects the interplay among photon helicity, phonon polarization, and ferro-rotational order.

    💡 亮点：本文利用共振非弹性 X 射线散射研究 MnTiO3 中与铁旋转序耦合的圆偏振声子，即铁轴向声子。作者发现，这些激发表现出非互易圆二色性：当入射 X 射线的传播方向反转时，二色响应会改变，尽管体系整体同时保持反演和时间反演对称性。结果表明，RIXS 中的圆二色性不仅受样品对称性影响，还与散射几何密切相关。

    可扩展电子自旋搬运的原子尺度无序约束#
    Constraints on Atomistic Disorder for Scalable Electron Spin Shuttling#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Raphaël J. Prentki, Pericles Philippopoulos, Mohammad Reza Mostaan, Félix Beaudoin

    📄 摘要：电子自旋搬运是指由栅极控制的、电子在量子比特寄存器之间的相干传输，它能够增加量子比特连通性并支持高效的量子纠错方案。它正成为可扩展硅自旋量子比特量子计算的关键使能技术。随着电子在微米尺度上运动，它会遇到埃尺度的无序，导致其约束势、谷分裂和谷相位发生波动。这些效应会引起向谷激发态的泄漏，从而限制高保真搬运的速度。因此，对搬运保真度的准确预测需要能够连接原子尺度与介观物理的建模工具。我们开发了一套多尺度仿真流程，用于量化这些效应在实验实现的 Si/SiGe “QuBus” 输运带架构中的表现。首先，我们使用有限元方法求解泊松方程，解析随时间变化、由栅极控制的器件静电势。其次，我们构建具有真实原子尺度无序（随机合金化和界面粗糙度）的输运带原子结构；并采用 Keating 价键力场模型在原子尺度上解析应变。第三，我们进行位置跟踪的原子尺度紧束缚模拟，以获得被搬运电子的时间依赖谷分裂和谷相位。最后，我们将这些时间轨迹参数化为一个随时间变化的薛定谔方程，并求解以预测谷动力学。我们发现，界面粗糙会强烈抑制搬运保真度，并在原子层尺度附近出现一个尖锐异常。总体而言，我们的预测为在硅自旋量子比特架构中实现可扩展、高保真的搬运提供了实用且定量的指南。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2510.03113v2 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: Electron spin shuttling, the gate-controlled, coherent transport of electrons between qubit registers, increases qubit connectivity and enables efficient quantum-error-correction schemes. It is emerging as a key enabler of scalable silicon spin-qubit quantum computing. As an electron travels over micrometers, it encounters angstrom-scale disorder, causing fluctuations in its confinement potential, valley splitting, and valley phase. These lead to leakage into the valley-excited state, limiting high-fidelity shuttling speeds. Accurate predictions of shuttling fidelities thus require modeling tools that link atomistic and mesoscopic physics. We develop a multiscale simulation workflow to quantify these effects in the experimentally-realized Si/SiGe "QuBus" conveyor-belt architecture. First, we resolve the time-dependent, gate-controlled device electrostatics by solving the Poisson equation using the finite-element method. Second, we construct conveyor belt atomic structures with realistic atomistic disorder (random alloying and interface roughness); we resolve strain atomistically using the Keating valence force-field model. Third, we perform position-tracked atomistic tight-binding simulations of the shuttled electrons to obtain their time-dependent valley splittings and phases. Finally, these time traces parametrize a time-dependent Schrödinger equation, which we solve to predict valley dynamics. We find that interface roughness strongly suppresses shuttling fidelities, with a sharp anomaly near the atomic-layer scale. Overall, our predictions set practical, quantitative guidelines to realize scalable, high-fidelity shuttling in silicon spin-qubit architectures.

    💡 亮点：电子自旋搬运是指利用栅极控制，在量子比特寄存器之间相干传输电子，这可提升量子比特连通性并支持高效量子纠错。本文针对实验实现的 Si/SiGe “QuBus” 输运带架构，建立了一个多尺度模拟流程，以量化原子级无序对搬运保真度的影响。结果表明，界面粗糙会强烈压低搬运保真度，并在原子层尺度附近出现明显异常，从而为实现可扩展、高保真硅自旋量子比特搬运提供了定量指导。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 声子、有限温度和输运性质对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    在扭转过渡金属二硫族化物中构建可调的 Kagome 莫尔超晶格#
    Engineering Tunable Kagome Moiré Superlattices in Twisted Transition Metal Dichalcogenides#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Adrian Fedorko, Chao-Xing Liu, Zhen Bi

    📄 摘要：Kagome 体系由于其独特的电子结构和几何挫折，是研究强关联相的理想平台。尽管近期的固体实现揭示了丰富的关联态，但它们存在一些关键局限，包括载流子密度和相互作用强度的可调性有限。本文提出一种实验上可行的方案：利用扭转三层过渡金属二硫族化物（TMD）实现 breathing kagome 莫尔超晶格。通过使顶层和底层分别相对于中间层以小角度 θ 和 2θ 扭转，我们在中心层上生成了一个类 Kagome 的莫尔势。连续模型计算显示，体系形成了孤立的 Kagome 能带，具有平带、带隙可调的狄拉克点以及范霍夫奇点。关键在于，这一平台可通过扭转角和静电栅控实现对能带结构、载流子密度和相互作用的前所未有的控制。我们的工作开辟了一条实现洁净、可调 Kagome 金属的新途径，并为研究强关联与拓扑现象提供了一个多功能平台。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2503.13422v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: Kagome systems are an ideal platform for exploring strongly correlated phases due to their unique electronic structure and geometric frustration. While recent solid-state realizations have uncovered a wealth of correlated states, they suffer from key limitations, including limited tunability of carrier density and interaction strength. Here, we propose an experimentally viable scheme to realize a breathing kagome moiré superlattice using a twisted trilayer of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs). By twisting the top and bottom layers relative to the middle by small angles θ and 2θ, respectively, we generate a kagome-like moiré potential on the central layer. Continuum model calculations reveal isolated kagome bands featuring flat bands, Dirac points with tunable gaps, and van Hove singularities. Crucially, this platform offers unprecedented control over band structure, carrier density, and interactions, achievable via twist angle and electrostatic gating. Our work opens a new route to realizing clean, tunable kagome metals and provides a versatile platform for studying strongly correlated and topological phenomena.

    💡 亮点：本文提出一种在扭转三层过渡金属二硫族化物中实现 breathing kagome 莫尔超晶格的实验可行方案。通过让上下两层相对中间层分别以小角度 θ 与 2θ 扭转，中心层上会形成类 Kagome 的莫尔势，从而得到孤立的 Kagome 能带，包括平带、可调能隙的狄拉克点和范霍夫奇点。该平台可通过扭转角和静电栅压同时调控能带结构、载流子密度与相互作用，为研究强关联与拓扑现象提供了新的洁净平台。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入非绝热分子动力学、电子—声子耦合、Wannier/GW-BSE 和载流子输运。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及AI for science 与材料/物理问题的结构化分析对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“先用高精度电子结构和声子/电子—声子矩阵元建立小规模基准，再训练可迁移 Hamiltonian 或 ML 势，最后在长时间尺度非绝热 MD、输运或界面电荷转移中验证”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    超导准粒子轨道磁矩的半经典理论#
    Semiclassical theory for the orbital magnetic moment of superconducting quasiparticles#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Jian-Hua Zeng, Zhongbo Yan, Zhi Wang, Qian Niu

    📄 摘要：我们采用半经典方法研究超导体中 Bogoliubov 准粒子的轨道磁矩。通过考虑波包能量在磁场线性阶的修正，我们推导了准粒子波包的轨道磁矩。随后，借助完整量子力学方法的线性响应计算验证了这一半经典结果。由解析表达式可知，仅有超导配对能隙的非平庸结构并不足以产生准粒子的轨道磁矩，这与准粒子 Berry 曲率的行为形成鲜明对比。我们将该公式应用于一个具有手性 d 波超导能隙的紧束缚模型，展示了轨道磁矩对能谱和局域态密度的影响。我们还计算了由 Bogoliubov 准粒子轨道磁矩与 Berry 曲率共同驱动的轨道 Nernst 效应。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2602.17376v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: We study the orbital magnetic moment of Bogoliubov quasiparticles in superconductors with the semiclassical approach. We derive the orbital magnetic moment of a quasiparticle wavepacket by considering the energy correction of the wavepacket to the linear order of the magnetic field. The semiclassical result is further verified by a linear response calculation with a full quantum mechanical method. From the analytical expression we find that nontrivial structure in the superconducting pairing gap alone is unable to produce quasiparticle orbital magnetic moment, which is in sharp contrast to the behavior of quasiparticle Berry curvatures. We apply the formula to study a tight-binding model with chiral d-wave superconducting gap, and show the influence of orbital magnetic moment on the energy spectrum and local density of states. We also calculate the orbital Nernst effect driven by the interplay between the orbital magnetic moment and the Berry curvature of Bogoliubov quasiparticles.

    💡 亮点：本文采用半经典方法研究超导体中 Bogoliubov 准粒子的轨道磁矩。作者推导了准粒子波包在磁场一阶修正下的轨道磁矩，并用完整量子力学的线性响应计算加以验证。结果表明，仅凭超导配对能隙的非平庸结构并不能产生准粒子的轨道磁矩，这与准粒子 Berry 曲率的行为形成鲜明对比。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    量子计算机上散射相移的量子力学：非厄米系统与虚时间模拟#
    Scattering phase shift in quantum mechanics on quantum computers: non-Hermitian systems and imaginary-time simulations#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Peng Guo, Paul LeVan, Frank X. Lee, Yong Zhao

    📄 摘要：为克服文献 Ref.i̧teGuo:2026qkx 中在实时间量子模拟里提取散射相移时所遇到的相关函数快速振荡行为，本文提出并测试了两种解决方案。一种是在虚时间中模拟厄米系统，另一种是在实时间中模拟非厄米系统。我们证明，这两种方法都会导致非幺正量子演化问题，而这一问题可以通过结合两种量子算法来解决：块编码和 Hadamard 测试。这个组合量子算法不需要中途电路测量，也不需要调整哈密顿量的输入参数，而且可以很容易地在量子计算机上实现。量子模拟器上的数值测试表明，在统计涨落淹没信号之前，两种方法都能在足够长的时间内与精确解一致。这些结果预示着，利用非厄米和虚时间模拟可以规避实时间模拟中固有的振荡，并可推广到其他量子系统。尤其是，与虚时间方法相比，非厄米方法在所需辅助量子比特数量上具有决定性优势，因此更便于扩展。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2604.00127v2 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: To overcome the fast oscillatory behavior of correlation functions for extracting scattering phase shift in real-time quantum simulations encountered in Ref.i̧teGuo:2026qkx, we propose and test two solutions in the present work. One is to simulate Hermitian systems in imaginary time, the other is to simulate non-Hermitian systems in real time. We demonstrate that both approaches lead to the problem of non-unitary quantum evolution which can be solved by combining two quantum algorithms: block encoding and Hadamard test. The combined quantum algorithm does not require mid-circuit measurements or adjustment of the input parameters of the Hamiltonian, and can be easily implemented on quantum computers. Numerical tests on quantum simulators show that both approaches agree with exact solutions for a sufficiently long time before the signal is lost in statistical fluctuations. The results bode well for using non-Hermitian and imaginary-time simulations to circumvent oscillations inherent in real-time simulation of other quantum systems. In particular, the non-Hermitian approach shows a decisive advantage over the imaginary-time one on the number of required ancillary qubits, and hence is more practical to scale up.

    💡 亮点：为克服实时间量子模拟中用于提取散射相移的关联函数快速振荡问题，本文提出并检验了两种方案：一是用虚时间模拟厄米系统，二是用实时间模拟非厄米系统。作者表明，这两种方法都会带来非幺正量子演化问题，但可通过块编码与 Hadamard 测试两种量子算法的结合来解决。数值实验显示，在统计涨落淹没信号之前，两种方法都能与精确解在足够长时间内一致，其中非厄米方法在所需辅助量子比特数量上更具优势。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，摘要目前只能确认：有效哈密顿量或机器学习哈密顿量。需要回到原文核对数据来源、标签定义、模型架构、物理约束和验证集，不能把题名直接等同于可迁移算法。若要接入团队的 ML 势或 Hamiltonian 流程，应先确认它是否同时学习能量、力、应力、自旋、极化或电子结构，以及是否报告跨材料/跨温度外推。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    🔗 相关工作关联：与团队方向的可能连接在于：AI for science 与材料/物理问题的结构化分析。团队已有机器学习势、第一性原理、有效 Hamiltonian、缺陷计算、电子—声子耦合和有限温度动力学基础，但该文是否提供可复用数据或物理量仍需查证。若研究对象属于机器人、量子信息或电网等非材料场景，关联主要是物理约束学习、少样本建模或不确定性评估，而不是直接迁移材料机制。这种联系应通过同一材料体系上的独立基准和跨分布测试确认，不能仅凭方法名称或期刊来源下结论。

    💡 对你方向的启示：对《量子计算机上散射相移的量子力学：非厄米系统与虚时间模拟》的启示是先做可证伪的对接：从一个已有材料体系和小规模 DFT 基准开始，明确输入、目标、基线和外推测试，再决定是否接入铁电/磁性、缺陷或载流子动力学工作。具体可先把该文的表示或损失函数在 HfO₂、二维滑移铁电、CrI₃/CrSBr、SiC/GaN 缺陷或卤化物钙钛矿上做小规模复现；若没有直接交叉，应保留为方法参考而非强行迁移。第一步可以选取团队已有的 HfO₂、二维磁体、半导体缺陷或钙钛矿数据做小样本试验，再决定是否扩大到生产级计算。

    文献研究：FedDOSE: Federated Learning Framework Decomposing Site Effects for Modeling Brain Dynamic Functional Connectivity#
    FedDOSE: Federated Learning Framework Decomposing Site Effects for Modeling Brain Dynamic Functional Connectivity#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Deepank Girish, Yi Hao Chan, Yubin Zheng, Sukrit Gupta, Jagath C. Rajapakse
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.07393v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging ( fMRI ) data are often pooled into collaborative multi-site consortia, as deep learning models for analyses require large datasets to generalize well. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative training, standard approaches continue to struggle with statistical heterogeneity. In particular, site differences pose a key challenge in multi-site data settings. Additionally, existing FL approaches for fMRI rely on static Functional Connectivity ( FC), omitting dynamic information in brain networks. To address this, we propose FedDOSE, a novel framework that explicitly decomposes site differences for analysis of dynamic FC (dFC). FedDOSE introduces a Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition block to encode high-dimensional dFC tensors and capture modular-level spatio-temporal patterns efficiently. Class-specific prototypes are generated across all sites and subsequently aligned at the global level by using a combination of Optimal Transport (OT) barycenter formulation and Procrustes analysis. Extensive experiments for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) and Attention-Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) on three multi-site resting-state fMRI datasets: ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II, and ADHD-200, demonstrate that FedDOSE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in ASD and ADHD detection. Our results highlight its effectiveness in learning robust representations from multi-site datasets for reliable analysis.

    面向瓷砖化 Ansatz 的低成本可扩展量子误差缓解#
    Cost-effective scalable quantum error mitigation for tiled Ansätze#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / physics👤 Oskar Graulund Lentz Rasmussen, Erik Kjellgren, Peter Reinholdt, Stephan P. A. Sauer, Sonia Coriani, Karl Michael Ziems, Jacob Kongsted🔥 AI 88

    📄 摘要：我们提出一种低成本量子误差缓解技术，它建立在近期提出的基于 Ansatz 的门误差与读出误差缓解方法（M0）之上。该技术称为 tiled M0，利用瓷砖化 Ansatz（例如 tUPS、QNP、硬件高效电路）的独特结构，对 M0 施加局域性近似，从而使噪声表征的 QPU 成本呈指数级降低。我们以 LiH、分子氢、水、丁二烯和苯（4–12 个量子比特）的 tUPS Ansatz 分子基态能量计算验证了该技术，并在有噪声模拟中证明其相较于 M0 几乎没有精度损失或损失极小。我们还展示了该技术在量子实验中的表现，凸显了其在近未来应用中的潜在用途。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2511.21236v2 Announce Type: replace-cross Abstract: We introduce a cost-effective quantum error mitigation technique that builds upon the recent Ansatz-based gate and readout error mitigation method (M0). The technique, tiled M0, leverages the unique structure of tiled Ansätze (e.g., tUPS, QNP, hardware-efficient circuits) to apply a locality approximation to M0 that results in an exponential reduction in the QPU cost of the noise characterization. We validate the technique for molecular ground state energy calculations with the tUPS Ansatz on LiH, molecular hydrogen, water, butadiene, and benzene (4-12 qubits), demonstrating little to no loss in accuracy compared to M0 in noisy simulations. We also show the performance of the technique in quantum experiments, highlighting its potential use in near-term applications.

    💡 亮点：本文提出一种建立在近期 Ansatz 基门误差与读出误差缓解方法 M0 之上的低成本量子误差缓解技术 tiled M0。该方法利用瓷砖化 Ansatz 的局部结构，对噪声表征成本进行指数级降低，同时在若干分子基态能量计算与量子实验中保持了接近 M0 的精度。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，摘要目前只能确认：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法。需要回到原文核对数据来源、标签定义、模型架构、物理约束和验证集，不能把题名直接等同于可迁移算法。若要接入团队的 ML 势或 Hamiltonian 流程，应先确认它是否同时学习能量、力、应力、自旋、极化或电子结构，以及是否报告跨材料/跨温度外推。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    利用超摩擦光阱中的冷里德堡原子进行射频场量子传感#
    Quantum sensing of radiofrequency fields using cold Rydberg atoms in a super-molasses trap#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / physics👤 Romain Granier, Anthony El Bekai, Miguel Angel Cifuentes Marín, Cédric Blanchard, Nassim Zahzam, Yannick Bidel, Alexandre Bresson, Vilius Atkočius, Chester Camm, Florence Concepcion, Konstantinos Karakostas, Matt Himsworth, Alexander Jantzen, Alexis Bonnin, Sylvain Schwartz🔥 AI 60

    📄 摘要：我们展示了基于超摩擦光阱中冷里德堡原子的射频场量子传感，且不需要磁线圈。我们的方法将冷原子的计量学优势与无金属介电传感头相结合，后者可最大限度减小对电磁环境的扰动，而这一特性此前仅限于基于蒸汽池的器件。此外，由于不存在感性负载，可以在冷却阶段与里德堡激发之间快速切换，从而得到比传统磁光阱窄一个数量级的阱损耗光谱信号。这使得自校准微波功率测量具有前所未有的 43 dB 动态范围，为校准测量应用开辟了新的前景。我们还报告了优于 1% 的比例因子线性度、在数十分钟内无漂移并由此达到 3 μV/cm 的分辨率，以及恢复所施加微波场椭圆率的可能性。通过在紧凑介电传感头中展示冷原子计量平台，本工作为利用里德堡原子进行射频测量领域的新应用，以及基于冷原子的其他量子传感领域如磁测量、重力测量或惯性导航铺平了道路。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.07260v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: We demonstrate the quantum sensing of radiofrequency fields based on cold Rydberg atoms in a super-molasses trap, without the need for magnetic coils. Our approach combines the metrological advantages of cold atoms with a metal-free dielectric sensor head minimizing perturbations to the electromagnetic environment, a feature that was previously restricted to vapor-cell-based devices. Moreover, the absence of inductive loads allows to rapidly alternate between the cooling phase and the Rydberg excitation, leading to trap-loss spectroscopy signals one order of magnitude narrower than for conventional magneto-optical traps. This enables self-calibrated microwave power measurements with an unprecedented dynamic range of 43dB, opening the door to new perspectives of applications in calibration measurements. We also report a scale factor linearity better than 1%, the absence of drifts over several tens of minutes leading to a 3μV/cm resolution, and the possibility to retrieve the ellipticity of the applied microwave field. By demonstrating a cold-atom metrological platform in a compact dielectric sensor head, this work paves the way for new applications in the field of radiofrequency measurements with Rydberg atoms, and in other fields of quantum sensing based on cold atoms such as magnetometry, gravimetry or inertial navigation.

    💡 亮点：本文展示了一种基于超摩擦光阱中冷里德堡原子的射频场量子传感方法，无需磁线圈。该方案结合冷原子的计量优势与无金属介电传感头，减少对电磁环境的扰动，并实现了窄谱信号和大动态范围微波功率自校准测量。结果显示其具有优良线性、低漂移和微弱场分辨率，并可恢复微波场椭圆率，为紧凑型冷原子射频测量及其他量子传感应用奠定基础。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    超越标准量子极限的纠缠增强光学磁测量#
    Entanglement-enhanced optical magnetometry beyond the standard quantum limit#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / physics👤 Jun Jia, Túlio Brito Brasil, Maimouna Bocoum, Andrea Grimaldi, Laurits Møberg, Mikhail Balabas, Jörg Helge Müller, Emil Zeuthen, Eugene Simon Polzik🔥 AI 7

    📄 摘要：光学原子磁测量是连续传感应用中的强大工具；然而，在没有量子关联的情况下，其灵敏度受到标准量子极限（SQL）的限制，该极限源自光学探针不精确性与量子测量反作用之间的折衷。超越 SQL 的灵敏度需要能够改变这些测量噪声来源的量子关联。在这里，我们通过使用探针光的纠缠态，并通过工程化测量不精确性与反作用之间的关联，展示了这种灵敏度。在首先在宽广频率范围内探索 SQL 之后，我们通过将变分读出与把磁力计耦合到二分纠缠光态的一个模式相结合，并根据另一个纠缠模式对结果进行条件化，展示了对该极限的超越。通过调谐被探测光的正交分量并组合来自两个测量通道的信号，我们在宽广的声学频率范围内实现了超越 SQL 的灵敏度，而该范围迄今为止仍是量子噪声受限光学磁测量难以触及的。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.06815v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Optical atomic magnetometry is a powerful tool for continuous sensing applications, yet, in the absence of quantum correlations, its sensitivity is limited by the standard quantum limit (SQL) stemming from a trade-off between optical probe imprecision and quantum measurement backaction. Beyond-SQL sensitivity requires quantum correlations that modify these measurement noise sources. Here we demonstrate such sensitivity by using entangled state of the probe light and by engineering correlations between measurement imprecision and backaction. Having first explored SQL in a broad range of frequencies, we demonstrate overcoming the limit by combining variational readout with coupling the magnetometer to one mode of a bipartite entangled light state and conditioning the results on the other entangled mode. Tuning the detected light quadratures and combining the signals from the two measurement channels, we achieve sensitivity beyond the SQL in a broad range of acoustic frequencies which has so far remained inaccessible to quantum-noise-limited optical magnetometry.

    💡 亮点：本文展示了通过量子关联增强连续光学原子磁测量灵敏度并超越标准量子极限。研究利用双模纠缠光态中的一模耦合磁力计，并以另一纠缠模进行条件化，同时结合变分读出和测量通道信号组合。结果在宽广声学频率范围内实现了超越 SQL 的灵敏度，该频段此前对量子噪声受限光学磁测量而言难以达到。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    电磁谐振器的模态分析：MAN 软件扩展至二维材料与耦合系统#
    Modal analysis of electromagnetic resonators: MAN software expansion to 2D materials and coupled systems#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / physics👤 Thomas Christopoulos, Tong Wu, P. Lalanne

    📄 摘要：本文介绍了此前发布的自由软件 MAN（Modal Analysis of Nanoresonators）[Comput Phys Commun 284, 108627 (2023)] 的更新版本。该软件包旨在高效计算并归一化准正则模（QNMs），并利用这些模对电磁谐振器的光学响应进行分析。本版本通过三项主要发展显著扩展了原软件的功能。首先，它引入了用于包含二维材料（包括石墨烯）的谐振器的专用模型和数值工具，采用面电导率表述。其次，它实现了耦合 QNM 形式主义，可直接从各个谐振器的 QNM 计算谐振器模之间的复耦合系数。与传统耦合模理论不同，后者中的这些系数通常以现象学方式引入或通过拟合确定，而 MAN 则从第一性原理严格地评估它们。第三，它提供了后处理工具，可根据已计算的 QNM 场分布直接确定控制消光光谱的 Fano 参数。综合这些进展，MAN 可处理的纳米光子系统范围得到了拓展，同时也提供了用于定量解释复杂共振现象的新工具。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2604.14328v2 Announce Type: replace Abstract: This work presents an updated version of the previously released freeware MAN (Modal Analysis of Nanoresonators) [Comput Phys Commun 284, 108627 (2023)], a software package designed to efficiently compute and normalize quasinormal modes (QNMs) and to use them for the analysis of the optical response of electromagnetic resonators. This release substantially extends the capabilities of the original software through three major developments. First, it introduces dedicated models and numerical tools for resonators incorporating two-dimensional materials, including graphene, using a surface-conductivity formulation. Second, it implements a coupled-QNM formalism that computes the complex coupling coefficients between resonator modes directly from the QNMs of the individual resonators. Unlike conventional coupled-mode theory, where these coefficients are often introduced phenomenologically or determined by fitting, MAN evaluates them rigorously from first principles. Third, it provides post-processing tools that determine the Fano parameters governing extinction spectra directly from the computed QNM field distributions. Together, these advances broaden the range of nanophotonic systems accessible to MAN while providing new tools for the quantitative interpretation of complex resonant phenomena.

    💡 亮点：本文介绍了此前发布的自由软件 MAN（Modal Analysis of Nanoresonators）的更新版本，显著扩展了其对准正则模及光学响应分析的能力。新版本增加了二维材料与石墨烯的专门模型、基于准正则模的耦合模处理，以及直接从场分布提取 Fano 参数的后处理工具，从而拓展了可研究的纳米光子系统范围。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，这篇工作可归入磁性机器学习势、自旋 Hamiltonian、自旋—晶格动力学和非共线磁结构。从摘要能确认的线索包括：论文题目和摘要中可确认的方法，以及磁性、自旋以及自旋—晶格耦合, 晶体结构、功能材料和结构—性质关系对应的结构或物理量。若迁移到团队流程，应采用“训练集同时覆盖原子位移和自旋构型，显式标注交换、各向异性、DMI 或自旋力；用自旋 MD/MC 与 DFT 自旋能差验证温度、应变和缺陷下的磁序稳定性”的分层验证，而不是只比较单点能量或单一回归误差。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：Phase closure in three dimensions and electromagnetic coupling#
    Phase closure in three dimensions and electromagnetic coupling#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 Preprints👤 Artur Novais🔥 AI 72
    📖 英文原文

    We propose a three-dimensional definition of phase closure based on a geometric consistency condition linking 2D phase closure to 3D mode overlap for two intersecting spheres. The spheres represent the relational limits of quantum-phase influence, with radii set by the two-body separation alone. Imposing that a relativistic two-dimensional phase invariant equals the ratio of total to interacting modes in the 3D geometry yields, at tree level, a parameter-free constraint whose only geometrically admissible solution is ((α₁⁻¹=137.190)), within ((0.11%)) of the observed fine structure constant and obtained independently of any assumed value or dynamical property. Higher-order geometric corrections with coefficients fixed by the same two-sphere geometry give ((α⁻¹=137.035999177)) through fourth order, agreeing at the ((0.7σ)) level with the determination of ((α)) from the electron magnetic moment (((g-2))), the most precise value independent of the atomic-recoil measurements. The construction is not derived from QED and reproduces the Schwinger term ((aₑ₌α/2π)) as an additional consistency check. The fifth-order term produces a two-tier prediction: at ((10⁻⁹)) absolute uncertainty, the series requires ((α⁻¹=137.035999177(5))); at ((10⁻¹⁰)) resolution, it predicts ((α⁻¹=137.0359991768(5))). Values outside either interval would falsify the series at the respective precision. This provides a direct experimental test of whether electromagnetic coupling is constrained by geometric phase coherence in three dimensions.

    💡 亮点：摘要信息不足，需查阅原文确认具体方法与结论。

    文献研究：Stochastic Hamiltonian Modulation of Energy-Marked Quantum States: An Ensemble-Based Numerical Assessment#
    Stochastic Hamiltonian Modulation of Energy-Marked Quantum States: An Ensemble-Based Numerical Assessment#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 Preprints👤 Abhinandan Bhatt, N. P. Bhosale, Vineet Mehan🔥 AI 5
    📖 英文原文

    Noise-assisted quantum dynamics can produce useful transport and detection effects, but conclusions drawn from individual stochastic trajectories may not persist under ensemble averaging. We assess a closed-system model in which selected computational-basis states are assigned unit energy in a diagonal Hamiltonian and coupled by a transverse term whose scalar amplitude is independently sampled from a zero-mean Gaussian distribution at each timestep. Four configurations containing 2, 3, and 10 qubits were simulated using sparse exponential propagation. For each original parameter setting, 100 seeded trajectories were compared with zero-perturbation and constant-coupling controls. At the originally proposed weak perturbation strengths, none of the four configurations showed a statistically resolved increase in final marked-state population: all 95% confidence intervals included the uniform baseline. Perturbation-strength sweeps revealed configuration-dependent responses at larger amplitudes, including increased population for one three-qubit configuration, but no universal marked-state amplification or established stochastic-resonance regime. The marked set must also be supplied when constructing the Hamiltonian, so eliminating an explicit phase-oracle circuit does not eliminate its information-encoding role. These results recast the model as an exploratory study of stochastically modulated energy-marked dynamics rather than an oracle-free search algorithm. The work demonstrates the importance of seeded ensembles, controls, and operational detection criteria when evaluating noise-assisted quantum-computing proposals.

    💡 亮点：摘要信息不足，需查阅原文确认具体方法与结论。

    文献研究：Emergent unconventional superconductivity in an aged α-Sn thin film#
    Emergent unconventional superconductivity in an aged α-Sn thin film#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 Preprints👤 Le Duc Anh, Tomoki Hotta, Daiki Nishigaki, Takahiro Chiba, Yohei Kota, Masaaki Tanaka🔥 AI 0
    📖 英文原文

    Superconductivity in topological materials offers a promising route to topological superconductors, which host robust exotic states for low-power spintronics and quantum devices. Here we report the emergence of intrinsic, unconventional superconductivity in a topologically nontrivial α-Sn thin film that develops twenty months after growth. This superconducting state exhibits a large in-plane critical magnetic field beyond the Pauli limit and an unusual twofold-symmetric angular dependence, consistent with type-II Ising superconductivity in centrosymmetric α-Sn. Magnetotransport measurements, Shubnikov–de Haas oscillations and first-principles calculations further reveal the appearance of an additional Fermi-surface band with linear dispersion, enhanced mobility and a relatively large effective mass in the superconducting phase. Our results suggest that α-Sn is a possible promising platform for topological superconductivity and show that aging-driven elemental diffusion and electronic reconstruction can trigger a quantum phase transition in an elemental Dirac semimetal.

    💡 亮点：摘要信息不足，需查阅原文确认具体方法与结论。

    文献研究：Engineering Spin-Wave Spectra through Longitudinal Compositional Interfaces#
    Engineering Spin-Wave Spectra through Longitudinal Compositional Interfaces#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 Preprints👤 Felipe Sarria, Eduardo Saavedra, Alejandro Pereira, Juan Escrig
    📖 英文原文

    Engineering spin-wave spectra without modifying device geometry remains a central challenge in magnonics. Here, we investigate how longitudinal compositional interfaces reshape the equilibrium magnetic configurations and spin-wave dynamics of homogeneous and multisegmented Co/CoNi/Ni nanowires using micromagnetic simulations. We demonstrate that compo-sitional interfaces reorganize the magnetic energy landscape, stabilizing hybrid magnetic configurations that reorganize the spin-wave eigenmode spectrum through mode hybridization, localization, and spectral-weight transfer. Rather than generating additional resonances, increasing the interface density continuously shifts the dominant microwave response from the C band of Ni nanowires toward the Ku band of Co nanowires, while multisegmented architectures naturally stabilize resonances within the technologically relevant X band. These findings establish longitudinal compositional interfaces as an independent design parameter for programming spin-wave spectra and engineering reconfigurable magnonic and microwave nanodevices.

    基于 EXAFS 与机器学习原子间势的 hcp 锌局域晶格动力学#
    Local lattice dynamics of hcp zinc from EXAFS and machine-learning interatomic potentials#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Vitalijs Dimitrijevs, Pjotrs Žguns, Inga Pudza, Aleksandr Kalinko, Alexei Kuzmin🔥 AI 97

    📄 摘要：我们结合温度依赖的 Zn K 边扩展 X 射线吸收精细结构（EXAFS）谱和原子模拟，研究了六方密堆积（hcp）锌这一典型各向异性金属的晶格动力学。通过反向蒙特卡罗方法，我们提取了八个配位壳层的均方相对位移（MSRD），从而给出了按壳层分辨的热运动描述。对 MSRD 温度依赖性的相关爱因斯坦模型分析，得到了有效原子间力常数，并揭示了面内与面外相互作用之间明显的各向异性。这种各向异性还可通过第一和第二配位壳层 MSRD 之比进一步量化，其结果与衍射实验中的各向异性位移参数高度一致。采用通用机器学习原子间势 CHGNet 的分子动力学模拟表明，原始模型会高估热无序，而经过微调的版本则显著改善了与实验 EXAFS 谱和径向分布函数的符合程度。总体而言，基于 EXAFS 的分析对于验证和改进机器学习原子间势是有效的。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.07081v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: The lattice dynamics of hexagonal close-packed (hcp) zinc, a prototypical anisotropic metal, is studied using temperature-dependent Zn K-edge extended X-ray absorption fine structure (EXAFS) spectroscopy combined with atomistic simulations. The reverse Monte Carlo method enable the extraction of mean-square relative displacements (MSRDs) for eight coordination shells, providing a shell-resolved description of thermal motion. The MSRD temperature dependence, analysed using the correlated Einstein model, yields effective interatomic force constants and reveals pronounced anisotropy between in-plane and out-of-plane interactions. This anisotropy is further quantified by the ratio of MSRDs for the first and second coordination shells, which closely matches the anisotropic displacement parameters from diffraction experiments. Molecular dynamics simulations using the CHGNet universal machine-learning interatomic potential show that the original model overestimates thermal disorder, while a fine-tuned version substantially improves agreement with experimental EXAFS spectrum and radial distribution function. Overall, EXAFS-informed analysis is effective for validating and refining machine-learning interatomic potentials.

    💡 亮点：本文结合温度依赖的 Zn K 边扩展 X 射线吸收精细结构（EXAFS）谱与原子模拟，研究了六方密堆积（hcp）锌这一典型各向异性金属的晶格动力学。通过反向蒙特卡罗方法提取了八个配位壳层的均方相对位移，从壳层分辨的角度描述热运动。基于相关爱因斯坦模型分析的结果表明，面内与面外相互作用之间存在明显各向异性。

    🔬 与我们研究方向的关系

    📐 方法要点：方法上，摘要目前只能确认：分子动力学与有限温度轨迹、蒙特卡洛统计采样。需要回到原文核对数据来源、标签定义、模型架构、物理约束和验证集，不能把题名直接等同于可迁移算法。若要接入团队的 ML 势或 Hamiltonian 流程，应先确认它是否同时学习能量、力、应力、自旋、极化或电子结构，以及是否报告跨材料/跨温度外推。还需核对原文的训练数据规模、损失函数、边界条件和误差分解，才能判断其是否适合团队的材料模拟任务。

    文献研究：Cross-Geometry Transferability Assessment of Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials: From Bulk Materials to Atomic Nanowires#
    Cross-Geometry Transferability Assessment of Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials: From Bulk Materials to Atomic Nanowires#
    🧭 材料 / 器件📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Pedro H. M. Zanineli, Bruno Focassio, Gabriel R. Schleder🔥 AI 98
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.06662v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Foundation machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable atomistic simulations at substantially lower computational cost than first-principles methods, but their reliability across structural geometries remains insufficiently understood. Here, we construct a density-functional-theory dataset of ZrO2 configurations spanning bulk, slab, particle, neck, and atomically thin wire environments motivated by an experimentally observed ZrO2 desintering process involving neck thinning and atomic wire formation. We first benchmark 26 pretrained MLIPs and observe pronounced geometry-dependent degradation in zero-shot predictions. Without any training, after only reference-energy alignment, the best zero-shot model (ORB-V3) reaches energy and force root-mean-square errors of 6 meV/atom and 197.3 meV/Å, respectively, with the largest force errors in neck and wire configurations. We then compare zero-shot inference, fine-tuning, and training from scratch strategies. Fine-tuning yields lower energy and force errors than training from scratch, while both require comparable wall-clock time. Geometry-specific fine-tuning improves in-domain accuracy but frequently produces negative transfer to other structural classes, whereas mixed-geometry fine-tuning reduces cross-geometry errors. Evaluations of elastic and vibrational properties, surface energies, and neck dynamics further show that rankings based on average energy and force errors do not universally predict property-level behavior. These results demonstrate that geometry-diverse target data and independent physical validations are necessary when adapting foundation MLIPs to low-coordination (ionic) nanostructures.

    💡 亮点：摘要信息不足，需查阅原文确认具体方法与结论。

    张量网络核机器：用于机器学习与非线性系统辨识的 JAX 框架#
    Tensor Network Kernel Machines: A JAX Framework for Machine Learning and Nonlinear System Identification#
    🧭 材料 / 器件📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Albert Saiapin, Kim Batselier🔥 AI 62

    📄 摘要：开发既具表达能力又计算高效的非线性模型，仍然是机器学习和非线性系统辨识中的一项挑战。张量网络核机器（TNKM）通过将非线性特征表示与紧凑的低秩张量网络参数化相结合来应对这一挑战。然而，用于开发 TNKM 模型的实用且可扩展的软件框架仍然有限。在这项工作中，我们介绍“tnkm”，一个使用 JAX 构建和训练 TNKM 模型的开源 Python 库。该库提供了一个统一接口，用于组合不同的特征映射、张量网络架构和优化策略，包括交替最小二乘法和基于梯度的方法。我们在非线性基准问题上展示了“tnkm”的能力，结果表明，所实现的模型在保持紧凑参数化和高效训练的同时，实现了具有竞争力的预测精度。所提出的框架促进了基于张量网络的学习方法的可复现开发与应用。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.07043v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: Developing nonlinear models that are both expressive and computationally efficient remains a challenge in machine learning and nonlinear system identification. Tensor network kernel machines (TNKM) address this challenge by combining nonlinear feature representations with compact low-rank tensor-network parameterizations. However, practical and extensible software frameworks for developing TNKM models remain limited. In this work, we introduce "tnkm", an open-source Python library for constructing and training TNKM models using JAX. The library provides a unified interface for combining different feature maps, tensor-network architectures, and optimization strategies, including alternating least squares and gradient-based methods. We demonstrate the capabilities of "tnkm" on nonlinear benchmark problems, showing that the implemented models achieve competitive prediction accuracy while retaining compact parameterizations and efficient training. The proposed framework facilitates reproducible development and application of tensor-network-based learning methods.

    💡 亮点：非线性模型既要有表达能力，又要计算高效，这在机器学习和非线性系统辨识中一直是难题。本文提出的 tnkm 通过将非线性特征表示与紧凑的低秩张量网络参数化相结合，来应对这一挑战。作者给出了一个基于 JAX 的开源 Python 库，用于构建与训练 TNKM 模型，并在非线性基准任务上验证了其有效性。

    扩散随机量子线路中局域关联函数的 KPZ 超扩散#
    KPZ Superdiffusion of Local Correlators in Diffusive Random Quantum Circuits#
    🧭 物理 / 凝聚态📖 arXiv / cond-mat👤 Ewan McCulloch🔥 AI 10

    📄 摘要：我们研究了一维粒子数守恒随机幺正线路与外部浴耦合时的单粒子格林函数 G(x,t)=〈σ⁻ₓ(0)σ⁺₀(t)〉。对于固定的时空无序，我们论证，在强噪声和弱噪声极限下，G(x,t) 都受随机介质中的有向波支配。我们在归一化空间分布 p(x,t)∝ |G(x,t)|² 的漂移统计及其相关自由能中发现 Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）标度。特别地，其中心 〈x(t)〉≡∑ₓ x p(x,t) 在 O(t²/³) 的长度尺度上漂移，而 -log∑ₓ |G(x,t)|² 的样本间涨落按 t¹/³ 标度增长。在弱噪声 γ≪1 下，向强无序不动点的交叉发生在参数上很长的时间 O(γ⁻³/²)。这些预言通过数值方法得到确认：在中等噪声下使用张量网络模拟单个线路中的含噪算符动力学，并在弱噪声下使用保留跃迁无序的相位退火代理模型。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.06459v1 Announce Type: cross Abstract: We study the single-particle Green's function G(x,t)=łangle σ⁻ₓ₍₀₎σ⁺₀₍ₜ₎ångle in one-dimensional particle-number-conserving random unitary circuits coupled to an external bath. For fixed spacetime disorder, we argue that G(x,t) is governed, in both the strong- and weak-noise limits, by directed waves in a random medium. We find Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) scaling in the wandering statistics of the normalized spatial distribution p(x,t)propto |G(x,t)|² and in the associated free energy. In particular, its center łangle x(t)ångleequivsumₓ x, p(x,t) wanders on a length-scale O(t²/³), while sample-to-sample fluctuations of -łogsumₓ |G(x,t)|² scale as t¹/³. At weak noise γ łl 1, the crossover to the strong-disorder fixed point occurs at a parametrically long time O(γ⁻³/²). These predictions are confirmed numerically using tensor-network simulations of the noisy operator dynamics in individual circuits at moderate noise, and of a phase-annealed proxy retaining hopping disorder at weak noise.

  58. 58
    从点到边：用于物理敏感型 PDE 学习的边条件谱算子#
    From Points to Edges: Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operators for Physics-Sensitive PDE Learning#
    🧭 材料 / 器件📖 arXiv / cs.AI👤 Zhentao Tan, Ruijie Quan, Yi Yang🔥 AI 0

    📄 摘要：神经算子已成为求解偏微分方程（PDE）的核心工具，其中谱算子能够在空间位置之间实现高效的全局混合。然而，许多 PDE 包含对底层物理行为至关重要的物理敏感局部结构。例如，在 Darcy 流中，局部材料界面通常会反映为渗透率场的突变，并且会强烈影响解。现有谱算子主要基于中心点表示来调节模态混合，因此对这类局部结构变化的响应不足。我们提出边条件谱算子（ESO），这是一种新的谱算子框架，它利用局部边级变化来调制全局谱混合。通过引入成对变化模态混合器（PVMM）将局部边信息注入谱模态选择，ESO 在保持谱神经算子全局逼近能力的同时，使学习到的核能够适应物理敏感的局部结构。此外，我们提出了任务自适应的物理感知重加权（PAR），利用任务特定的物理量强调物理上重要的区域。在九个 PDE 基准上，ESO 一致地取得了最先进的性能。可视化和区域级分析进一步表明，ESO 减少了系数突变、高梯度流动结构以及其他物理敏感区域附近的解误差。代码可在 https://github.com/Tanpig-X/ESO 获取。

    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.06894v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Neural operators have become a central tool for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), with spectral operators offering efficient global mixing across spatial locations. However, many PDEs contain physics-sensitive local structures that are critical to the underlying physical behavior. For example, in Darcy flow, local material interfaces are often reflected by sharp changes in the permeability field and can strongly influence the solution. Existing spectral operators primarily adapt modal mixing based on center-point representations, making them insufficiently responsive to such localized structural variations. We propose the Edge-Conditioned Spectral Operator (ESO), a novel spectral operator framework that modulates global spectral mixing using local edge-wise variations. By incorporating the Pairwise-Variation Modal Mixer (PVMM) to inject local edge information into spectral mode selection, ESO preserves the global approximation capability of spectral neural operators while enabling the learned kernel to adapt to physics-sensitive local structures. Furthermore, we introduce a task-adaptive Physics-Aware Reweighting (PAR) that emphasizes physically important regions, identified by taskspecific physical quantities. Across nine PDE benchmarks, ESO consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Visual and region-wise analyses further demonstrate that ESO reduces solution errors near coefficient jumps, high-gradient flow structures, and other physically sensitive regions. The code is available at https://github.com/Tanpig-X/ESO.

    💡 亮点：神经算子已成为求解偏微分方程的核心工具，其中谱算子能够在空间位置之间进行高效的全局混合。然而，许多 PDE 含有对物理行为至关重要的局部结构。现有谱算子主要基于中心点表示来调节模态混合，因此对这类局部结构变化响应不足。本文提出边条件谱算子 ESO，通过局部边级变化来调制全局谱混合，并引入任务自适应的物理感知重加权机制。

    文献研究：Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning#
    Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning#
    🧭 材料 / 器件📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Yao Wu
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.07349v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Learning from heterogeneous representations is usually reduced to feature concatenation, which erases which representation produced an error. We instead algebraize the residual: a representation is a typed object that owns both a coordinate system and the residual it leaves unresolved, and learning is an ordered composition of operators that preserve or deliberately erase that type. Fold realizes the objects as point-in-time conditional-mean fields on 10x10 rank grids. FPRC-PQ realizes the algebra as relax-aggregate-close: each field is relaxed by a correction fitted to its own residual in its own coordinates; corrected fields meet at a fixed mean that is the sole identity-erasure boundary; and a shared learner closes only the aggregate's fresh residual. The composition telescopes exactly into representation, local residual estimate, and residual-of-residual estimate. Its aggregate is a learned control-variate interface with population variance reduction, while refitting the closer along perturbations of the backbone yields first-order coupled-path mean orthogonality. As an analytical extension, a reflective rumination operator reads the displacement of a global reconstruction from the aggregate anchor, reflects it, and fixes its gain by a unique orthogonal projection rather than return-tuned grid search. On 3.67M Chinese A-share stock-day observations (2023-2026) under a frozen point-in-time protocol, the evaluated base algebra raises net-of-cost return from 13.52% to 19.10% and Sharpe from 1.42 to 2.09. Matched-capacity, unified-residual, identity-free two-stage, and pairwise-only controls all trail it. The gain is therefore not explained by more features or more trees, but by making residual ownership and composition explicit while representation identity is still available.

    💡 亮点：摘要信息不足，需查阅原文确认具体方法与结论。

    文献研究：TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning#
    TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning#
    🧭 材料 / 器件📖 arXiv / cs.LG👤 Yuhan Xie, Jingrong Huang, Chen Lyu
    📖 英文原文

    arXiv:2608.07274v1 Announce Type: new Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) facilitates privacy-preserving collaborative training with reduced client-side overhead. However, its split architecture introduces unique attack surfaces, rendering it vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks. Most existing defenses fail to exploit the split paradigm, limiting their ability to detect and contain malicious behaviors at an early stage. To bridge this gap, we propose Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling (TOFD), a unified framework that jointly enables proactive detection and robust optimization against a wide range of poisoning attacks. TOFD operates in three stages: (1) Target Inference, which identifies potential attack targets by refining class-wise safe zones via class-specific Margin Perturbation (MP); (2) Sample Purification, which adaptively filters poisoned smashed data using thresholds calibrated through cross-class min-max normalization of MP; and (3) Decoupling Optimization, which leverages an adversarial guidance model to capture attack-induced patterns and decouple their influence during optimization, thereby suppressing residual adversarial effects. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of TOFD. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that TOFD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses under diverse attack scenarios, achieving superior robustness with low computational overhead suitable for practical deployment.
